\displaystyle\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\varepsilon^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]:=\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}.

Proof.

Since
Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})=\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}[\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}(\hat{r}^{n}(s_{h},a_{h})+b^{n}(s_{h},a_{h}))]
and
Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})=\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}[\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}(r(s_{h},a_{h})+b^{n}(s_{h},a_{h}))]
,
we have

\displaystyle\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\left(\hat{r}^{n}(s_{h},a_{h})-r(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\left(\hat{r}^{n}(s_{h},a_{h})-r(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})^{\top}\left(\hat{\mu}^{n}-\mu^{*}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\left\|\hat{\mu}^{n}-\mu^{*}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{\textup{HF}}^{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}2\varepsilon^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right],

where inequality (a) is due to the definition of event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{cov}}
.
∎

Let

\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}:=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}]=2\varepsilon^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}[\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}]
,
W_{\theta}:=\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}-\frac{1}{1-\gamma}

and

W_{Q}:=\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
.

Lemma D.11
.

Assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\textup{cov}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\textup{MLE}}
holds. Then, for any phase
n\geq 0
, iteration
t\geq 0
,
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
and
a\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|\leq\sqrt{32\beta W_{Q}\varepsilon^{n}_{\textup{HF}}(n+1)\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}+W_{\theta}\sqrt{8\beta\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}.

Proof.

For any phase
n\geq 0
and iteration
t\geq 0
, for any fixed
\theta
and
(s,a)
, using Lemma
F.3
, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta\right)^{2}-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4W_{Q}\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|

\displaystyle\leq 4W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a},

(12)

where
W_{Q}
satisfies that
\max\{|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})|,|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})|,|\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta+b^{n}(s,a)|\}\leq W_{Q}
for all
n\geq 0
,
t\geq 0
and
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
.

Taking
\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[\cdot]
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta\right)^{2}\right]

(13)

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4W_{Q}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}\right]

\displaystyle=4W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}.

(14)

Plugging
\theta^{t}_{*}
into
\theta
, we have that for any fixed
(s,a)
,

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]-4W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\geq}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]-4W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}

(15)

where inequality (a) is due to the definition of
\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}
.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}4W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4W_{Q}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 8W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}},

(16)

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
F.3
.

On the other hand, it holds that

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)\phi(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right]}_{\textup{Term $\Gamma$}\ \geq\ 0},

(17)

where Term
\Gamma
is non-negative due to the the first-order optimality of
\theta^{t}_{*}
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 8W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}.

Since
\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[(\phi(s,a)^{\top}(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}))^{2}]=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})^{\top}\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})]=(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}](\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})
,
we have

\displaystyle(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}](\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})\leq 8W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\ \quad\left\|\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}

\displaystyle=\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d^{\pi^{i}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}\right]+\zeta_{\textup{cov}}I\right)\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{n}\sum_{(s,a)}d^{\pi^{i}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a)\cdot\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}+\zeta_{\textup{cov}}I\right)\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle=(n+1)\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{(s,a)}\frac{1}{n+1}\sum_{i=0}^{n}d^{\pi^{i}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a)\cdot\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}{n+1}I\right)\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle=(n+1)\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{(s,a)}\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}(s,a)\cdot\phi(s,a)\phi(s,a)^{\top}+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}{n+1}I\right)\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 8(n+1)W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4\zeta_{\textup{cov}}W_{\theta}^{2}.

For any
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
, using the definitions of
\mathcal{K}^{n}
and event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{cov}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{(\hat{\Sigma}^{n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\leq\sqrt{\beta}.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\left\|\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{\Sigma^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\beta\left(8(n+1)W_{Q}\varsigma^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4\zeta_{\textup{cov}}W_{\theta}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{32\beta W_{Q}\varepsilon^{n}_{\textup{HF}}(n+1)\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}+W_{\theta}\sqrt{8\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}.

∎

D.3.3
Elliptical Potential Analysis for Human Data

Lemma D.12

(Elliptical Potential for the Baseline Policy)
.

For any phase
n\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\left\|\phi(\tau)\right\|^{2}_{\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]\leq\frac{2d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\log(N).

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\left\|\phi(\tau)\right\|^{2}_{\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\phi(\tau)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{i}}_{\rho^{i-1}_{\textup{cov}}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)\left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)^{\top}\right]+\zeta_{\textup{HF}}I\right)^{-1}\phi(\tau)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{1}{c_{\textup{base}}}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\phi(\tau)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\phi(\tau^{(2)})\phi(\tau^{(2)})^{\top}\right]+\zeta_{\textup{HF}}I\right)^{-1}\phi(\tau)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{c_{\textup{base}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\frac{1}{n}\textup{tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{E}_{\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\phi(\tau^{(2)})\phi(\tau^{(2)})^{\top}\right]+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I\right)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\phi(\tau)\phi(\tau)^{\top}\right]\right)+\frac{W_{\tau}^{2}}{c_{\textup{base}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\frac{1}{n}+\frac{W_{\tau}^{2}}{c_{\textup{base}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\left(\log(N)+1\right)+\frac{1}{c_{\textup{base}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(b)}}{\leq}\frac{2d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\log(N).

where inequality (a) uses Assumption
3.4
, and inequality (b) holds if
\log(N)\geq 2
which can be easily guaranteed in our problem.
∎

Lemma D.13

(Elliptical Potential for Preference-based Data)
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}\right)\leq 2d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(1+\frac{4NW_{\tau}^{2}}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}d}\right)+\frac{2d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}(N)}{c_{\textup{base}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

For any phase
n\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sqrt{T\cdot\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sqrt{T^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}.

We make the convention that
n\Sigma^{n}_{\textup{HF}}:=\zeta_{\textup{HF}}I
for
n=0
.
Then, we obtain

\displaystyle\ \quad\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\sqrt{\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{\top}\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)}\right]}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sqrt{\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{\top}\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sqrt{N\cdot\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{\top}\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sqrt{N\cdot\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{\top}\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]}}

\displaystyle=N^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{\top}\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{\top}\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|^{2}_{\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

where inequality (a) uses the Jensen inequality.

It holds that

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|^{2}_{\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\left\|\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})+\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right\|^{2}_{\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\Bigg(2\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n+1}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\left\|\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right\|^{2}_{\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]+2\mathbb{E}_{\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\left\|\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right\|^{2}_{\left(n\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}2\sum_{n=1}^{N}\!\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{n}}_{\rho^{n-1}_{\textup{cov}}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\!\Bigg[\left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)^{\!\!\top}\!\!\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\!\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{i}}_{\rho^{i-1}_{\textup{cov}}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)\left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)^{\top}\right]\!+\!\zeta_{\textup{HF}}I\Bigg)^{\!\!-1}\!\!\!\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\ \left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)\Bigg]+\frac{4d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\log(N)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(b)}}{\leq}4d\log\Bigg(\frac{\det\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{i}}_{\rho^{i-1}_{\textup{cov}}},\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)\left(\phi(\tau^{(1)})-\phi(\tau^{(2)})\right)^{\top}\right]+\zeta_{\textup{HF}}I\bigg)}{\det\left(\zeta_{\textup{HF}}I\right)}\Bigg)\!+\!\frac{4d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\log(N)

\displaystyle\leq 4d\log\left(1+\frac{4NW_{\tau}^{2}}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}d}\right)+\frac{4d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\log(N).

Here inequality (a) uses Lemma
D.12
and Assumption
3.4
. Inequality (b) follows from the elliptical potential lemma (Lemma
F.5
) and the fact that
\zeta_{\textup{HF}}:=4W_{\tau}^{2}
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(4d\log\left(1+\frac{4NW_{\tau}^{2}}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}d}\right)+\frac{4d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\log(N)\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq 2d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(1+\frac{4NW_{\tau}^{2}}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}d}\right)+\frac{2d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}(N)}{c_{\textup{base}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

∎

D.3.4
Discussion on Assumption
3.4

Assumption
3.4
can be abstracted from the RLHF framework, and serve as a technical condition for an independent mathematical problem.

We provide Lemma
D.14
to demonstrate that under Assumption
3.4
, one can systematically utilize the elliptical potential lemma
(
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
to obtain a mathematical conclusion that is independent of the RLHF framework.

Our RLHF analysis (Lemmas
D.12
and
D.13
) is an application of this systematical analytical procedure.

Lemma D.14
.

Let
\Phi:=\{\phi\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\|\phi\|_{2}\leq W_{\phi}\}
. There are random distributions
\mathcal{D}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{D}_{N}
and
\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}
over
\Phi
, and a regularization parameter
\zeta\geq W_{\phi}^{2}
.

Assume that
\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}
satisfies that for any
n\in[N]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\phi\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi-\phi^{\prime})(\phi-\phi^{\prime})^{\top}\right]\succeq c_{\textup{base}}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}\phi^{\prime\top}\right]

(18)

for some constant
c_{\textup{base}}\in(0,1)
.

Then, we can use the elliptical potential lemma (Lemma
F.5
)
(
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
to bound

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{n=1}^{N}\bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi_{n}-\phi^{\prime}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{2}{c_{\textup{base}}}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi^{\prime}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}_{i}(\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 4d\log\left(1+\frac{NW_{\phi}^{2}}{\zeta d}\right)+\frac{2d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\left(\log(N)+2\right).

Proof.

According to the assumption Eq. (
18
), we have that for any
i\in[N]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\phi\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi-\phi^{\prime})(\phi-\phi^{\prime})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\succeq c_{\textup{base}}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}\phi^{\prime\top}\right]+\zeta I\succeq c_{\textup{base}}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}\phi^{\prime\top}\right]+c_{\textup{base}}\zeta I,

which implies that

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{E}_{\phi\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi-\phi^{\prime})(\phi-\phi^{\prime})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}\preceq\frac{1}{c_{\textup{base}}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}\phi^{\prime\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}.

Hence, we have that for any
v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle v^{\top}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\phi\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi-\phi^{\prime})(\phi-\phi^{\prime})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}v\leq\frac{1}{c_{\textup{base}}}v^{\top}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}\phi^{\prime\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}v.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi_{n}-\phi^{\prime}_{n}+\phi^{\prime}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\bigg(2\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi_{n}-\phi^{\prime}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +2\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi^{\prime}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}2\sum_{n=1}^{N}\bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi_{n}-\phi^{\prime}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{2}{c_{\textup{base}}}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\left\|\phi^{\prime}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}_{i}(\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}}\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{n=1}^{N}\textup{tr}\left(\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{i},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})(\phi_{i}-\phi^{\prime}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\zeta I\right)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{n},\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[(\phi_{n}-\phi^{\prime}_{n})(\phi_{n}-\phi^{\prime}_{n})^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{2}{c_{\textup{base}}}\sum_{n=2}^{N}\frac{1}{n-1}\cdot\textup{tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}(\phi^{\prime})^{\top}\right]+\frac{\zeta}{n-1}I\right)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\phi^{\prime}_{n}\sim\mathcal{D}_{\textup{base}}}\left[\phi^{\prime}_{n}(\phi^{\prime}_{n})^{\top}\right]\right)+\frac{2W_{\phi}^{2}}{c_{\textup{base}}\zeta}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(b)}}{\leq}4d\log\left(1+\frac{NW_{\phi}^{2}}{\zeta d}\right)+\frac{2d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\sum_{n=2}^{N}\frac{1}{n-1}+\frac{2W_{\phi}^{2}}{c_{\textup{base}}\zeta}

\displaystyle\leq 4d\log\left(1+\frac{NW_{\phi}^{2}}{\zeta d}\right)+\frac{2d}{c_{\textup{base}}}\left(\log(N)+2\right),

where inequality (a) uses Assumption
3.4
, and inequality (b) applies the elliptical potential lemma (Lemma
F.5
)
(
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
.

∎

D.4
Proof of Theorem
4.2

For any phase
n=0,\dots,N-1
and iteration
t=0,\dots,T-1
, define

\displaystyle\theta^{t}_{*}

\displaystyle:=\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\|\theta\|_{2}\leq W_{\theta}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}

\displaystyle:=\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\|\theta\|_{2}\leq W_{\theta}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle\theta^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{SGD}}{\approx}\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\|\theta\|_{2}\leq W_{\theta}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right].

For any
n\geq 0
,
t\geq 0
and
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
, let
\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a):=b^{n}(s,a)-\mathbb{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[b^{n}(s,a^{\prime})\right]
and
\bar{\phi}^{t}(s,a):=\phi(s,a)-\mathbb{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\phi(s,a^{\prime})\right]
.

Proof of Theorem
4.2
.

Using Lemma
D.5
, we have that for any phase
n=0,\dots,N-1
and iteration
t=0,\dots,T-1
,

\displaystyle\ \quad V_{\mathcal{M}^{n}}^{\pi^{*,n}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V_{\mathcal{M}^{n}}^{\pi^{t}}(s_{\textup{init}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[A_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\bigg[\hat{A}^{\pi^{t}}_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}(s,a)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\underbrace{\left(A_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\bar{\phi}^{t}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a)\right)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}}_{\textup{Term 1}}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\underbrace{\bar{\phi}^{t}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}}_{\textup{Term 2}}+\underbrace{\bar{\phi}^{t}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}}_{\textup{Term 3}}\bigg].

(19)

Following the proof of Lemma D.1 in
(
Agarwal et al. 2020
)
, we can bound Terms 1 and 3 as follows.

Term 1

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left(A_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\bar{\phi}^{t}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a)\right)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b(s,a)\right)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}},a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a^{\prime})-\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b(s,a^{\prime})\right)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*}}}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*}},a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a^{\prime})-\left(\phi(s,a^{\prime})^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a^{\prime})\right)\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{s_{\textup{init}}}^{\star}}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\phi(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon_{\textup{bias}}},

(20)

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
D.2
.

Define the Q-value function fitting error as

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{Q}

\displaystyle:=8W_{Q}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{SGD}}}}.

With probability at least
1-2NT\delta^{\prime}
,

Term 3

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d^{\pi^{*}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\bar{\phi}^{t}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}W_{\theta}^{2}+\beta(n+1)\varepsilon_{Q}}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}W_{\theta}^{2}+8\beta W_{Q}^{2}(n+1)\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{SGD}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq 2W_{\theta}\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}+4W_{Q}\sqrt{\beta(n+1)}\left(\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{SGD}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

(21)

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\theta}:=\left\{\textup{Term 3}\leq 2\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}W_{\theta}^{2}+\beta(n+1)\varepsilon_{Q}}\right\}.

Then,
\Pr[\mathcal{E}_{\theta}]\geq 1-2NT\delta^{\prime}
.

Now we have
\Pr[\mathcal{E}_{\theta}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\textup{MLE}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\textup{cov}}]\geq 1-4\cdot 2N(K+M_{\textup{HF}}+TM_{\textup{SGD}})\cdot 2\delta^{\prime}\geq 1-\delta
.
In the following, we assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\theta}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\textup{MLE}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\textup{cov}}
holds, and derive the suboptimality guarantee.

Applying Lemma
D.11
, Term 2 can be bounded as follows.

Term 2

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\bar{\phi}^{t}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}},a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\left|\phi(s,a^{\prime})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 16\sqrt{\beta W_{Q}\varepsilon^{n}_{\textup{HF}}(n+1)\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}+8W_{\theta}\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}.

(22)

Plugging the connection result between
\mathcal{M}^{n}
and
\mathcal{M}
(Lemma
D.3
) into the suboptimality decomposition (Eq. (
19
)), we have

\displaystyle\ \quad V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{t}}(s_{\textup{init}})\leq\textup{RHS in Eq.~\eqref{eq:regret_decomposition}}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{K}^{n}}d^{\pi^{t}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a).

Summing over
t=0,\dots,T-1
and dividing
T
, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{n+1}}(s_{\textup{init}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{t}}(s_{\textup{init}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\textup{RHS in Eq.~\eqref{eq:regret_decomposition}}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{K}^{n}}d^{\pi^{n+1}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{(1-\gamma)\eta T}+\frac{\eta W_{A}^{2}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon_{\textup{bias}}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{4W_{Q}\sqrt{\beta(n+1)}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{SGD}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{10W_{\theta}{\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}}}{16-\gamma}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\frac{16\sqrt{\beta W_{Q}}}{1-\gamma}\cdot 2\left(\frac{(n+1)^{2}\left(d+\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\right)}{c_{\textup{MLE}}^{2}M_{\textup{HF}}}+2(n+1)\zeta_{\textup{HF}}W_{\mu}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot

\displaystyle\ \quad\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{K}^{n}}d^{\pi^{n+1}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a),

(23)

where inequality (a) combines the natural policy gradient regret (Lemma
D.6
) and Terms 1-3 (Eqs. (
20
)-(
22
)).

Summing over
n=0,\dots,N-1
and dividing
N
, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{\textup{out}}}(s_{\textup{init}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\left(V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{n+1}}(s_{\textup{init}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{(1-\gamma)\eta T}+\frac{\eta W_{A}^{2}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon_{\textup{bias}}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{8W_{Q}\sqrt{\beta N}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{SGD}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{10W_{\theta}\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\frac{32\sqrt{\beta W_{Q}}}{1-\gamma}\cdot 2\left(\frac{4N^{2}\left(d+\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\right)}{c_{\textup{MLE}}^{2}M_{\textup{HF}}}+4N\zeta_{\textup{HF}}W_{\mu}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot

\displaystyle\ \quad\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\phi(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{\textup{HF}}^{n}\right)^{-1}}\right]}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sum_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{K}^{n}}d^{\pi^{n+1}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{(1-\gamma)\eta T}+\frac{\eta W_{A}^{2}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon_{\textup{bias}}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{8W_{Q}\sqrt{\beta N}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{SGD}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{10W_{\theta}\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\frac{256\sqrt{\beta W_{Q}}}{1-\gamma}\cdot\left(\frac{N^{2}\left(d+\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\right)}{c_{\textup{MLE}}^{2}M_{\textup{HF}}}+N\zeta_{\textup{HF}}W_{\mu}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\Bigg(d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(1+\frac{4NW_{\tau}^{2}}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}d}\right)+\frac{d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}(N)}{c_{\textup{base}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\ \quad+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)N}\cdot\frac{2d}{\beta}\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}d}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon_{\textup{bias}}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{W_{A}\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{T}}+\frac{8W_{Q}\sqrt{\beta N}}{1-\gamma}\cdot\frac{\log^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{(M_{\textup{SGD}})^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\frac{10W_{\theta}\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}}{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\frac{2\cdot 256\sqrt{\beta W_{Q}}}{1-\gamma}\cdot\frac{d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(5N\right)}{c_{\textup{base}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\cdot\left(\frac{2N^{2}d\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{c_{\textup{MLE}}^{2}M_{\textup{HF}}}+N\zeta_{\textup{HF}}W_{\mu}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{2d}{(1-\gamma)N\beta}\log\left(2N\right),

(24)

where inequality (a) uses Lemmas
D.13
,
D.4
, and the fact
\eta:=\frac{\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}}{W_{A}\sqrt{T}}
.

In addition, due to the condition of concentration event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{cov}}
, we should guarantee
K\geq\frac{16(N+1)^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}^{2}}
and
M_{\textup{HF}}\geq\frac{16W_{\tau}^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}
.

Recall that
W_{\tau}:=\frac{\log(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}})}{1-\gamma}
,
W_{\mu}:=1
,
W_{A}:=\frac{4}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
,
W_{\theta}:=\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}-\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
,
W_{Q}:=\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
,
c_{\textup{MLE}}:=\frac{1}{2+\exp(-2W_{\tau}W_{\mu})+\exp(2W_{\tau}W_{\mu})}
,
\xi:=\frac{W_{\theta}}{(W_{Q}+W_{\theta})\sqrt{T}}
,
\zeta_{\textup{cov}}:=1
and
\zeta_{\textup{HF}}:=4W_{\tau}^{2}=\frac{4\log^{2}(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
.

We set

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle:=\frac{6^{2}W_{A}^{2}\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{(1-\gamma)^{2}\varepsilon^{2}},

\displaystyle\eta

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}}{W_{A}\sqrt{T}}=\frac{(1-\gamma)\varepsilon}{6W_{A}^{2}},

\displaystyle\beta

\displaystyle:=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{5}\varepsilon^{5}c_{\textup{base}}}{5000\cdot 6^{5}\cdot 2^{4}\cdot 256^{4}W_{Q}^{2}W_{\mu}^{2}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}d^{2}}\log^{-2}\left(\frac{800\cdot 256^{2}d^{3}W_{Q}W_{\mu}\sqrt{10\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}}{(1-\gamma)^{4.5}\sqrt{c_{\textup{base}}}}\right)=\tilde{O}\left(\frac{(1-\gamma)^{5}\varepsilon^{5}c_{\textup{base}}}{W_{Q}^{2}W_{\mu}^{2}d^{2}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}\right),

\displaystyle N

\displaystyle:=\frac{6\cdot 10d}{(1-\gamma)\varepsilon\beta}\log\left(\frac{6\cdot 4d}{(1-\gamma)\varepsilon\beta}\right)=\tilde{O}\left(\frac{d^{3}W_{Q}^{2}W_{\mu}^{2}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}\varepsilon^{6}c_{\textup{base}}}\right),

\displaystyle M_{\textup{SGD}}

\displaystyle:=1200\cdot\underbrace{\frac{6^{4}\cdot 8^{4}\cdot W_{Q}^{4}\beta^{2}N^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}\varepsilon^{4}}}_{:=L_{1}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{L_{1}L_{2}^{2}L_{3}}{\delta}\right)=\tilde{O}\left(\frac{W_{Q}^{4}d^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}\varepsilon^{6}}\right),

\displaystyle M_{\textup{HF}}

\displaystyle:=1200\cdot\underbrace{\frac{6^{4}\cdot 2^{5}\cdot 256^{4}\beta^{2}W_{Q}^{2}N^{2}d^{2}\log(5N)}{(1-\gamma)^{4}\varepsilon^{4}c_{\textup{MLE}}^{2}c_{\textup{base}}}}_{:=L_{3}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{L_{1}L_{2}^{2}L_{3}}{\delta}\right)=\tilde{O}\left(\frac{W_{Q}^{2}d^{4}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}\varepsilon^{6}c_{\textup{MLE}}^{2}c_{\textup{base}}}\right),

\displaystyle K

\displaystyle:=\frac{64N^{2}}{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}^{2}}\cdot\log^{2}\underbrace{\left(\frac{4dN\cdot 12N(K+1+T)M_{\textup{HF}}M_{\textup{SGD}}}{\delta}\right)}_{:=L_{2}}=\tilde{O}\left(\frac{d^{6}W_{Q}^{4}W_{\mu}^{4}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{12}\varepsilon^{12}c_{\textup{base}}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\delta^{\prime}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\delta}{12N(K+1+T)M_{\textup{HF}}M_{\textup{SGD}}}.

(25)

Then, we have

\displaystyle V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{\textup{out}}}(s_{\textup{init}})\leq\varepsilon+\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon_{\textup{bias}}}}{1-\gamma}.

Finally, the number of samples is bounded by

\displaystyle\quad\ \tilde{O}\left(N\left(K+M_{\textup{HF}}+TM_{\textup{SGD}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\Bigg(\frac{W_{Q}^{2}W_{\mu}^{2}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}d^{3}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}\varepsilon^{6}c_{\textup{base}}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{W_{Q}^{4}W_{\mu}^{4}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{2}d^{6}}{(1-\gamma)^{12}\varepsilon^{12}c_{\textup{base}}^{2}}+\frac{W_{Q}^{2}d^{4}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}\varepsilon^{6}c_{\textup{MLE}}^{2}c_{\textup{base}}}+\frac{W_{A}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}\cdot\frac{W_{Q}^{4}d^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}\varepsilon^{6}}\bigg)\cdot\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\Bigg(\frac{W_{Q}^{6}W_{\mu}^{6}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{3}d^{9}}{(1-\gamma)^{19}\varepsilon^{18}c_{\textup{base}}^{3}}\Bigg).

(26)

∎

Appendix E
Proofs for PO-RLHF with Neural Function Approximation

In this section, we provide the proofs for algorithm
\mathtt{NN\mbox{-}PG\mbox{-}RLHF}
.

Definitions for Neural Function Approximation.

We first introduce or recall some definitions.

Let
\mathcal{S}_{R}:=\{w\in\mathbb{R}^{md}:\left\|w-w^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq R\}
and
\mathcal{U}_{R}:=\{\mu\in\mathbb{R}^{md}:\left\|\mu-\mu^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq R\}
.

For any
w\in\mathbb{R}^{md}
, recall that

\displaystyle[\psi_{w}]_{\ell}(s,a)

\displaystyle:=\frac{b_{\ell}}{\sqrt{m}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}>0\right\}\phi(s,a)\ \in\mathbb{R}^{d},\quad\forall\ell\in[m],

\displaystyle\psi_{w}(s,a)

\displaystyle:=\left[[\psi_{w}]_{1}(s,a);\dots;[\psi_{w}]_{m}(s,a)\right]\ \in\mathbb{R}^{md}.

Here
\underline{c}\leq\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}\|_{2}\leq\bar{c}
for all
\ell\in[m]
for some constants
\underline{c},\bar{c}>0
.

Recall the Q-network, policy network and reward network as follow:

\displaystyle f(s,a;\theta)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}b_{\ell}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\theta]_{\ell}>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\theta]_{\ell}=\psi_{\theta}(s,a)^{\top}\theta,

\displaystyle\pi_{\alpha,w}(a|s)

\displaystyle:=\frac{\exp\left(\alpha f(s,a;w)\right)}{\sum_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}\exp\left(\alpha f(s,a^{\prime};w)\right)}=\frac{\exp(\alpha\psi_{w}(s,a)^{\top}w)}{\sum_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}\exp\left(\alpha\psi_{w}(s,a^{\prime})^{\top}w\right)},

\displaystyle h(s,a;\mu)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}b^{\prime}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu]_{\ell}>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu]_{\ell}=\psi_{\mu}(s,a)^{\top}\mu.

For any
t\geq 0
, we use
\pi^{t}
and
\pi_{\alpha^{t},w^{t}}
interchangeably.

Let

\displaystyle f_{0}(s,a;w)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}b_{\ell}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}=\psi_{w^{0}}(s,a)^{\top}w,

\displaystyle h_{0}(s,a;\mu)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}b^{\prime}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu]_{\ell}=\psi_{\mu^{0}}(s,a)^{\top}w.

Define the neural kernel spaces as

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{w}_{R,\infty}:=\left\{f(s,a)=f(s,a;w^{0})+\int\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}w>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}\nu^{w}(w)dp^{w}(w):\ \left\|\nu^{w}(w)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{R}{\sqrt{d}}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,\infty}:=\left\{f(s,a)=h(s,a;\mu^{0})+\int\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}\mu>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}\nu^{\mu}(\mu)dp^{\mu}(\mu):\ \left\|\nu^{\mu}(\mu)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{R}{\sqrt{d}}\right\},

Here
\nu^{w}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
f(s,a;w^{0})
parameterize the element of
\mathcal{F}^{w}_{R,\infty}
, and
p^{w}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is the density function of the initialization distribution of
w^{0}
.
Similarly,
\nu^{\mu}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
h(s,a;\mu^{0})
parameterize the element of
\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,\infty}
, and
p^{\mu}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is the density function of the initialization distribution of
\mu^{0}
. In this work, for simplicity, we set the initialization distribution for
w^{0}
and
\mu^{0}
as
\mathcal{D}_{\textup{init}}
.

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,m}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}b^{\prime}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu]_{\ell}:\ \left\|\mu-\mu^{0}\right\|_{2}\leq R\right\},

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{F}}^{\mu}_{R,m}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}b^{\prime}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}\phi(s,a)^{\top}[\mu]_{\ell}:\ \left\|[\mu]_{\ell}-[\mu^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{R}{\sqrt{md}}\right\}.

\bar{\mathcal{F}}^{\mu}_{R,m}
is the subset of
\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,m}
.

Let
\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\in\mathcal{U}_{R}
be the parameter such that

\displaystyle\textup{Proj}_{\mathcal{F}_{R,m}}r(s,a)=\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}.

Covariance Matrix Concentration.

Next, we define the concentration event for the coverage and human data covariance matrices.

For any trajectory
\tau=(s_{0},a_{0},\dots,s_{H(\tau)},a_{H(\tau)})
and
\mu\in\mathcal{U}_{R}
, let
\psi_{\mu}(\tau):=\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{\mu}(s_{h},a_{h})
.
For any trajectories
\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)}
and
\mu\in\mathcal{U}_{R}
, let
\tilde{\psi}_{\mu}^{\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)}}:=\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)})}\psi_{\mu}(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h})-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)})}\psi_{\mu}(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h})
.

For any
n\geq 0
and
t\geq 0
, let
(s^{n}_{i},a^{n}_{i})
denote the
i
-th state-action pair sampled in phase
n
for constructing the estimated coverage covariance matrix
\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}
(Line
4
in Algorithm
3
).

For any phase
n\geq 0
, define

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{i=0}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\psi_{0}(s^{n}_{i},a^{n}_{i})\psi_{0}(s^{n}_{i},a^{n}_{i})^{\top}\right)+\zeta_{\textup{cov}}I,

\displaystyle\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{i=0}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d^{\pi^{i}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}\left[\psi_{0}(s,a)\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\right]+\zeta_{\textup{cov}}I

\displaystyle=(n+1)\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\right)+\zeta_{\textup{cov}}I.

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)^{\top}+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\tilde{\psi}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}_{0}\left(\tilde{\psi}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}_{0}\right)^{\top}+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I,\quad\forall n\geq 1

\displaystyle\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{i}}_{\rho^{i-1}_{\textup{cov}}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)})\right)\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)})\right)^{\top}\right]\Bigg)+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{i}}_{\rho^{i-1}_{\textup{cov}}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\tilde{\psi}^{\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)}}_{0}\left(\tilde{\psi}^{\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)}}_{0}\right)^{\top}\right]\Bigg)+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I,\quad\forall n\geq 1

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}

\displaystyle=\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}:=\zeta_{\textup{HF}}I.

Recall
W_{\tau}:=\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{1-\gamma}
and the definition of event
\mathcal{E}_{\tau}
(Eq. (
11
)).

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}:=\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\leq\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\leq 2\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}})}^{-1},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\leq\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\leq 2\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})}^{-1},\ \forall 0\leq n\leq N-1\Bigg\}.

Lemma E.1
.

Assuming that event
\mathcal{E}_{\tau}
holds, then we have
\Pr[\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}]\geq 1-2N\delta^{\prime}
.

Proof.

This lemma follows from Lemma
F.2
and the condition that
K\geq\frac{16(N+1)^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}^{2}}
and
M_{\textup{HF}}\geq\frac{16W_{\tau}^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}
. ∎

E.1
Neural Function Approximation

In the following, we present useful technical lemmas for neural function approximation.
Lemmas
E.2
-
E.5
borrow the ideas from prior neural network theory works
(
Rahimi & Recht 2008
;
Cai et al. 2019
;
Wang et al. 2019
;
Xu et al. 2021
)
.

For brevity of presentation, Lemmas
E.2
and
E.3
are written with parameter
w
and function
f
, but it works for parameters
w,\theta,\mu
and their corresponding functions
f,h
.

For ease of notation, we simplify the notations
\psi_{\theta^{0}}
and
\psi_{\mu^{0}}
as
\psi_{0}
, which can be easily recovered from the context.

Lemma E.2
.

For any
w,w^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{md}
such that
\|w-w^{0}\|_{2}\leq R
and
\|w^{\prime}-w^{0}\|_{2}\leq R
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}-\psi_{w}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}{\underline{c}\sqrt{m}},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)-\psi_{w}(s,a)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\textup{scale}}R}{\underline{c}\sqrt{m}}.

Proof.

We prove the first statement as follows.

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}-\psi_{w}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}b_{\ell}\cdot\left(\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}-\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}>0\right\}\right)\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{\prime}]_{\ell}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\left|\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}-\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}>0\right\}\right|\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{\prime}]_{\ell}\right|.

Since
\left|\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}-\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}>0\right\}\right|
implies

\displaystyle\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}-\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2},

we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}-\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}>0\right\}\right|\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}.

(27)

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}-\psi_{w}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{\prime}]_{\ell}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}\left(\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|+\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}\left([w^{\prime}]_{\ell}-[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\ \left(\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{\prime}]_{\ell}-[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right),

where inequality (a) is due to
\mathbbm{1}\left\{|x|\leq y\right\}|x|\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{|x|\leq y\right\}y
.

Using the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}-\psi_{w}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\left(2\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|[w^{\prime}]_{\ell}-[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4R^{2}}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4R^{2}}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}-\psi_{w}(s,a)^{\top}w^{\prime}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4R^{2}}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{4c_{\textup{scale}}R^{2}}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\frac{\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}}{\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4c_{\textup{scale}}R^{2}}{m}\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\frac{1}{\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}{\underline{c}\sqrt{m}},

where inequality (a) uses Assumption
3.3
.

Next, we prove the second statement using the similar argument.

\displaystyle\quad\ \left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)-\psi_{w}(s,a)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\frac{b_{\ell}^{2}}{m}\cdot\left(\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}>0\right\}-\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}>0\right\}\right)^{2}\left\|\phi(s,a)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{1}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\},

where inequality (a) uses Eq. (
27
).

Taking
\mathbb{E}_{\rho}[\cdot]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)-\psi_{w}(s,a)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left|\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right|\leq\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\textup{scale}}}{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\frac{\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}}{\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\textup{scale}}}{m}\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}-[w]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\frac{1}{\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\textup{scale}}R}{m}\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\frac{1}{\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\textup{scale}}R}{\underline{c}\sqrt{m}}.

∎

Lemma E.3
.

For any
w\in\mathcal{S}_{R}
and
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\|\psi_{w}(s,a)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 1,

\displaystyle\|w\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R,

\displaystyle|f(s,a;w)|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\|\psi_{w}(s,a)\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\|[\psi_{w}(s,a)]_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}}=\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\frac{b_{\ell}^{2}}{m}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\phi(s,a)^{\top}[w]_{\ell}>0\right\}\|\phi(s,a)\|_{2}^{2}}\leq 1.

In addition,

\displaystyle\left\|w^{0}\right\|_{2}=\sqrt{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\left\|[w^{0}]_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\leq\sqrt{m}\bar{c}.

Then,

\displaystyle\left\|w\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|w^{0}\right\|_{2}+\left\|w-w^{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle|f(s,a;w)|=|\psi_{w}(s,a)^{\top}w|\leq\left\|\psi_{w}(s,a)\right\|_{2}\left\|w\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R.

∎

Lemma E.4

(Projection Error for
\bar{\mathcal{F}}^{\mu}_{R,m}

(
Rahimi & Recht 2008
)
)
.

Let
h\in\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,\infty}
. For any
\delta^{\prime}>0
, with probability at least
1-\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\textup{Proj}_{\bar{\mathcal{F}}_{R,m}}h-h\right\|_{\rho}\leq\frac{R\left(1+\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\right)}{\sqrt{m}},

where
\rho
is a distribution over
\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
.

Lemma E.5

(Distance between
r(s,a)
and
\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}
)
.

Assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}
holds. Then,

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{0}^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}-r\right\|_{\rho}\leq 4R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{m}}.

Proof.

Recall that
r\in\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,\infty}
and
\textup{Proj}_{\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,m}}r(s,a)=\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}
.
Since
\bar{\mathcal{F}}^{\mu}_{R,m}
is a subset of
\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{R,m}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{0}^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}-r\right\|_{\rho}=\left\|\textup{Proj}_{\mathcal{F}_{R,m}}r-r\right\|_{\rho}\leq\left\|\textup{Proj}_{\bar{\mathcal{F}}_{R,m}}r-r\right\|_{\rho}\leq 4R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{m}}.

∎

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}:\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{0}^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}-r\right\|_{d^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\leq 4R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{m}},\ \forall t\in[T],\ \forall n\in[N]\Bigg\}.

Lemma E.6
.

It holds that
\Pr[\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}]\geq NT\delta^{\prime}
.

Proof.

This lemma follows from Lemma
E.5
and a union bound.
∎

E.2
Neural Neural Policy Gradient

Let
W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}:=\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R
.
According to Remark 28 in
(
Agarwal et al. 2021
)
, since
\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\|_{2}\leq 1
,
\log(\pi_{\alpha,w})
is a smooth function with smoothness parameter
W_{S}=1
.

Lemma E.7

(Neural Neural Policy Gradient)
.

For any phase
n\geq 0
and iteration
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left(\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}+\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]\leq\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{\eta}+\eta W_{S}(W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta})^{2}T.

Proof.

Following the analysis in
(
Agarwal et al. 2021
)
, according to the
W_{S}
-smoothness of
\log(\pi_{\alpha^{t},w^{t}})
, we have

\displaystyle\log\left(\pi_{\alpha^{t+1},w^{t+1}}(a|s)\right)-\log\left(\pi_{\alpha^{t},w^{t}}(a|s)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\

\displaystyle\nabla_{w}\log\left(\pi_{\alpha^{t},w^{t}}(a|s)\right)^{\top}\left(\alpha^{t+1}w^{t+1}-\alpha^{t}w^{t}\right)-W_{S}\left\|\alpha^{t+1}w^{t+1}-\alpha^{t}w^{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

For any
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad\textup{KL}(\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)\|\pi^{t}(\cdot|s))-\textup{KL}(\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)\|\pi^{t+1}(\cdot|s))

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)}\left[\log\left(\frac{\pi^{*,n}(a|s)}{\pi^{t}(a|s)}\right)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)}\left[\log\left(\frac{\pi^{*,n}(a|s)}{\pi^{t+1}(a|s)}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)}\left[\log\left(\frac{\pi^{t+1}(a|s)}{\pi^{t}(a|s)}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)}\left[\nabla_{w}\log\left(\pi_{\alpha^{t},w^{t}}(a|s)\right)^{\top}\left(\alpha^{t+1}w^{t+1}-\alpha^{t}w^{t}\right)-W_{S}\left\|\alpha^{t+1}w^{t+1}-\alpha^{t}w^{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\eta\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)}\left[\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}\right]-\eta^{2}W_{S}\left\|\theta^{t}\right\|_{2}^{2},

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)}\left[\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}\right]\leq\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\textup{KL}(\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)\|\pi^{t}(\cdot|s))-\textup{KL}(\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)\|\pi^{t+1}(\cdot|s))\right)+\eta W_{S}\left\|\theta^{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

For any phase
n\geq 0
,
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
and
a\in\mathcal{A}
, we have
b^{n}(s,a)=0
, and then
\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a):=b^{n}(s,a)-\mathbb{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[b^{n}(s,a^{\prime})\right]=0
.

Adding
s\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}
on both sides and summing over
t=0,\dots,T-1
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left(\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}+\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\textup{KL}(\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)\|\pi^{0}(\cdot|s))-\textup{KL}(\pi^{*,n}(\cdot|s)\|\pi^{T}(\cdot|s))\right]+\eta W_{S}(W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta})^{2}T

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{\eta}+\eta W_{S}(W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta})^{2}T.

∎

Algorithm 5

Q-network Training via Projected SGD (with the objective Eq. (
10
))

1:

Input:

f(s,a;w^{0})
,
\xi_{\theta}
.

2:

for

i=0,\dots,M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1

do

3:

g^{t,i}\leftarrow 2\left(f(s_{i},a_{i};\theta^{t,i})-\left(\hat{Q}^{\pi^{t}}(s_{i},a_{i};\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s_{i},a_{i})\right)\right)\nabla_{\theta}f(s_{i},a_{i};\theta^{t,i})
, where
(s_{i},a_{i})\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}
and
\hat{Q}^{\pi^{t}}(s_{i},a_{i};\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})
is estimated by Monte Carlo sampling

4:

\tilde{\theta}^{t,i+1}:=\theta^{t,i}-\xi_{\theta}g^{t,i}

5:

\theta^{t,i+1}\leftarrow\textup{Proj}_{\mathcal{U}_{R}}(\tilde{\theta}^{t,i+1})

6:

end

for

7:

return

\theta^{t}=\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\theta^{t,i}

E.3
Q-value Function Fitting

For any fixed phase
n=0,\dots,N-1
and fixed iteration
t=0,\dots,T-1
, define

\displaystyle F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta)

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(f_{0}(s,a;\theta)-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}

\displaystyle:=\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\theta\in\mathcal{S}_{R}}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta).

Then,

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta)

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[2\left(f_{0}(s,a;\theta)-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)\nabla_{\theta}f_{0}(s,a;\theta)\right].

Furthermore, for any
i=0,\dots,M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1
, define

\displaystyle g^{t,i}

\displaystyle:=2\left(f(s_{i},a_{i};\theta^{t,i})-\left(\hat{Q}^{\pi^{t}}(s_{i},a_{i};\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s_{i},a_{i})\right)\right)\nabla_{\theta}f(s_{i},a_{i};\theta^{t,i}),

\displaystyle\tilde{\theta}^{t,i+1}

\displaystyle:=\theta^{t,i}-\xi_{\theta}g^{t,i},

\displaystyle\bar{g}^{t,i}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[2\left(f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right],

and it holds that

\displaystyle\theta^{i+1}=\textup{Proj}_{\mathcal{S}_{R}}(\tilde{\theta}^{i+1}).

Let
W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}:=\frac{4}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{4(\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R)}{1-\gamma}
.

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}:=\Bigg\{\bigg|\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}(g^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}(\bar{g}^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\bigg|\leq 2W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}R\sqrt{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)},

\displaystyle\forall 0\leq n\leq N-1,\forall 0\leq t\leq T-1,\forall 0\leq i\leq M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1\Bigg\}.

Lemma E.8
.

It holds that
\Pr[\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}]\geq 1-2NT\delta^{\prime}
.

Proof.

This lemma can be obtained by using the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality and the union bound.
∎

Let
W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}:=\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R
,
W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}:=\frac{\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
and
\xi_{\theta}:=\frac{R}{W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}\sqrt{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}}
.

Below we give the guarantee for the projected SGD of Q-network training, which is described in algorithm
5
.

Lemma E.9

(SGD for Q-value Function Fitting)
.

Assume that event
\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}
holds. Then, for any phase
n\geq 0
and iteration
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t})-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle\leq 4W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{12R^{2}(W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}+W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q})\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}:=\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}.

Proof.

Fix phase
n
and iteration
t
.
For any
i=0,\dots,M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1
, since
F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta)
is convex with respect to
\theta
, we have

\displaystyle F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle\leq\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle=(g^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)+\left(\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-g^{t,i}\right)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\xi_{\theta}}(\theta^{t,i}-\tilde{\theta}^{t+1,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)+\left(\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-g^{t,i}\right)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\xi_{\theta}}\left(\left\|\theta^{t,i}-\tilde{\theta}^{t,i+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\tilde{\theta}^{t,i+1}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-g^{t,i}\right)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{\theta}}{2}\left\|g^{t,i}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\xi_{\theta}}\left(\left\|\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\theta^{t,i+1}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-g^{t,i}\right)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

Summing
i=0,\dots,M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1
and dividing
M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t})-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle=F\left(\frac{1}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\theta^{t,i}\right)-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{1}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{\theta}}{2M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left\|g^{t,i}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\xi_{\theta}M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\left(\left\|\theta^{t,0}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\theta^{t,M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left(\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-\bar{g}^{t,i}+\bar{g}^{t,i}-g^{t,i}\right)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{\theta}}{2M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left\|g^{t,i}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{R^{2}}{2\xi_{\theta}M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}+\frac{1}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left(\bar{g}^{t,i}-g^{t,i}\right)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{2R}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-\bar{g}^{t,i}\right\|_{2},

(28)

where inequality (a) uses the Jensen inequality.

For any
i\geq 0
, let
\mathcal{H}_{i}
be all histories of steps
0,\dots,i
, and we make the convention that
\mathcal{H}_{i-1}=\emptyset
for
i=0
. Let
\mathbb{E}_{i}[\cdot|\mathcal{H}_{i-1}]
denote the expectation with respect to the randomness at step
i
conditioning on all histories of steps
0,\dots,i-1
.
Then, for any
i\geq 0
, we have
\mathbb{E}_{i}[\nabla_{\theta}\hat{F}^{i}(\theta^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)|\mathcal{H}_{i-1}]=\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)
.

According to the definition of event
\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}(g^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)-\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}(\bar{g}^{t,i})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t,i}-\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}R\sqrt{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}.

(29)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left\|\nabla_{\theta}F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,i})-\bar{g}^{t,i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\Big[2\left(f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)\nabla_{\theta}f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})

\displaystyle\quad\ -2\left(f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\Big]\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\Big[\left\|f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\nabla_{\theta}f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\left\|\nabla_{\theta}f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\Big[\left\|f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\nabla_{\theta}f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\left\|f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\left\|\nabla_{\theta}f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-\nabla_{\theta}f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\Big[\left|f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\ +(W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}+W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q})\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)-\psi_{\theta^{t,i}}(s,a)\right\|_{2}\Big].

(30)

Plugging Eqs. (
29
) and (
30
) into Eq. (
28
), we have

\displaystyle F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t})-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle\leq 4W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{4R}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{i=0}^{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\Big[\left|f_{0}(s,a;\theta^{t,i})-f(s,a;\theta^{t,i})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\ +(W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}+W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q})\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)-\psi_{\theta^{t,i}}(s,a)\right\|_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}4W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+4R\left(\frac{2\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\frac{(W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}+W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q})\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{12R^{2}(W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}+W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q})\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}},

where inequality (a) uses Assumption
3.3
.

∎

E.4
Human Feedback

Recall that for any trajectories
\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)}
and
\mu\in\mathcal{U}_{R}
, let
\tilde{\psi}_{\mu}^{\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)}}:=\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)})}\psi_{\mu}(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h})-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)})}\psi_{\mu}(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h})
,
\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)};\mu):=\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)})}h(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h};\mu)-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)})}h(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h};\mu)
and
\tilde{r}(\tau^{(1)},\tau^{(2)}):=\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)})}r(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h})-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)})}r(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h})
.

For any fixed phase
n=0,\dots,N-1
, define the approximated MLE objective function and its optimal solution as follows:

\displaystyle L(\mu)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\Bigg(-\log\Bigg(\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}}{1+\exp\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)}_{i})}f_{0}(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h};\mu)-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)}_{i})}f_{0}(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h};\mu)\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)}_{i})}f_{0}(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h};\mu)-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)}_{i})}f_{0}(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h};\mu)\right)}\Bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(-\log\left(\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left((\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}

\displaystyle:=\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\mu\in\mathcal{U}_{R}}L(\mu).

Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\Bigg(\underbrace{\Bigg(-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)}\Bigg)}_{:=q^{i}_{0}(\mu)}\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\Bigg),

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu}^{2}L(\mu)

\displaystyle\!=\!\!\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\!\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\!\left(\left(\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)}{\left(1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)\right)^{2}}\!+\!\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu}{\left(1+\exp\left((\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)\right)^{2}}\right)\!\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\!\!(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\!\!\right)\!.

For any
j=0,\dots,M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1
, define

\displaystyle z^{j}

\displaystyle:=\left(-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{j}=1\right\}\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j})\right)}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j})\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{j}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j})\right)}\right)\nabla_{\mu}\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j}),

\displaystyle\bar{z}^{j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\Bigg(\underbrace{\Bigg(-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}\Bigg)}_{:=q^{i}(\mu^{j})}\nabla_{\mu}\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\Bigg),

\displaystyle\tilde{\mu}^{j+1}

\displaystyle:=\mu^{j}-\xi_{\mu}z^{j},

where
(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j},y_{j})
is uniformly drawn from
\{(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i},y_{i})\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}
.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mu^{j+1}=\textup{Proj}_{\mathcal{U}_{R}}(\tilde{\mu}^{j+1}).

Algorithm 6

Reward Network Training via Projected SGD (with the objective Eq. (
9
))

1:
