Input:

h(s,a;\mu^{0})
,
\xi_{\mu}
.

2:

for

j=0,\dots,M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1

do

3:

z^{j}\leftarrow\left(-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{j}=1\right\}\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j})\right)}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j})\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{j}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j})\right)}\right)\nabla_{\mu}\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j};\mu^{j})

4:

\tilde{\mu}^{j+1}\leftarrow\mu^{j}-\xi_{\mu}z^{j}

5:

\mu^{i+1}\leftarrow\textup{Proj}_{\mathcal{U}_{R}}(\tilde{\mu}^{j+1})

6:

end

for

7:

return

\hat{\mu}^{n}=\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\mu^{j}

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}:=\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\nabla_{\mu}\hat{L}^{j}(\mu^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)-\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq 8W_{\tau}R\sqrt{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)},\ \forall 0\leq n\leq N-1,\forall 0\leq j\leq M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1\Bigg\}.

Lemma E.10
.

It holds that
\Pr[\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}]\geq 1-2N\delta^{\prime}
.

Proof.

This lemma can be obtained by applying the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality and the union bound.
∎

Let
\xi_{\mu}:=\frac{R}{W_{\tau}\sqrt{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}
.

Below we provide the guarantee for the projected SGD of reward training, which is illustrated in algorithm
6
.

Lemma E.11

(SGD for the Reward Model)
.

Assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}
holds. Then, for any phase
n
,

\displaystyle\quad\ L(\mu^{n})-L(\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}})

\displaystyle\leq 17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{2R}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\Bigg(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\bigg(2\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +4W_{\tau}\left|\tilde{h}_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right|\bigg)\Bigg):=\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{40R^{2}W_{\tau}\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

For any
j=0,\dots,M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1
,

\displaystyle L(\mu^{j})-L(\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}})

\displaystyle\leq\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)

\displaystyle=(z^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)+\left(\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})-z^{j}\right)^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\xi_{\mu}}\left(\mu^{j}-\tilde{\mu}^{j+1}\right)^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)+\left(\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})-z^{j}\right)^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\xi_{\mu}}\left(\left\|\mu^{j}-\tilde{\mu}^{j+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\tilde{\mu}^{j+1}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\left(\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})-z^{j}\right)^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{\mu}}{2}\left\|z^{j}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\xi_{\mu}}\left(\left\|\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\mu^{j+1}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\left(\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})-z^{j}\right)^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right).

Summing
j=0,\dots,M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1
and dividing
M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ L(\mu^{n})-L(\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}})

\displaystyle=L\left(\frac{1}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\mu^{j}\right)-L(\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{1}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left(L(\mu^{j})-L(\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{\mu}}{2M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left\|z^{j}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\xi_{\mu}M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\left(\left\|\mu^{0}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\mu^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{1}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left(\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})-\bar{z}^{j}+\bar{z}^{j}-z^{j}\right)^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\xi_{\mu}}{2M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left\|z^{j}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{R^{2}}{2\xi_{\mu}M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}+\frac{1}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left(\bar{z}^{j}-z^{j}\right)^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{2R}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})-\bar{z}^{j}\right\|_{2},

(31)

where inequality (a) uses the Jensen inequality.

We have

\displaystyle\left\|z^{j}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\tilde{\psi}^{\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j}}_{\mu^{j}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=2\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)}_{j})}\psi_{\mu^{j}}(s^{(1)}_{j,h},a^{(1)}_{j,h})-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)}_{j})}\psi_{\mu^{j}}(s^{(2)}_{j,h},a^{(2)}_{j,h})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4W_{\tau}.

(32)

For any
j\geq 0
, let
\mathcal{H}_{j}
be all histories of steps
0,\dots,j
, and we make the convention that
\mathcal{H}_{j-1}=\emptyset
for
j=0
. Let
\mathbb{E}_{j}[\cdot|\mathcal{H}_{j-1}]
denote the expectation with respect to the randomness at step
j
(i.e.,
(\tau^{(1)}_{j},\tau^{(2)}_{j},y_{j})\sim\textup{Unif}(\{(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i},y_{i})\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}})
) conditioning on all histories of steps
0,\dots,j-1
. Then, for any
j\geq 0
, we have
\mathbb{E}_{j}[\nabla_{\mu}\hat{L}^{j}(\mu^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)|\mathcal{H}_{j-1}]=\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)
.

According to the definition of
\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}(z^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)-\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}(\bar{z}^{j})^{\top}\left(\mu^{j}-\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 8W_{\tau}R\sqrt{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}.

(33)

We have

\displaystyle\left|q^{i}_{0}(\mu^{j})-q^{i}(\mu^{j})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{j}\right)}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{j}\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{j}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}-\frac{\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{j}\right)}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{j}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\ +\left|\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{j}\right)}--\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}2\left|\tilde{h}_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right|,

where inequality (a) uses the fact that the derivative of functions
\frac{\exp(x)}{1+\exp(x)}
and
\frac{1}{1+\exp(x)}
lies in
(0,1)
.

Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\quad\ \left\|\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{j})-\bar{z}^{j}\right\|_{2}

(34)

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(q^{i}_{0}(\mu^{j})\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-q^{i}(\mu^{j})\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left\|q^{i}_{0}(\mu^{j})\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-q^{i}_{0}(\mu^{j})\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}+q^{i}_{0}(\mu^{j})\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-q^{i}(\mu^{j})\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(2\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\right\|_{2}+2W_{\tau}\left|q^{i}_{0}(\mu^{j})-q^{i}(\mu^{j})\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(2\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\right\|_{2}+4W_{\tau}\left|\tilde{h}_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right|\right).

(35)

Plugging Eqs. (
32
)-(
35
) into Eq. (
31
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\ L(\mu^{n})-L(\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}})

\displaystyle\leq 8\xi_{\mu}W_{\tau}^{2}+\frac{R^{2}}{2\xi_{\mu}M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}+8W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{2R}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\Bigg(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\bigg(2\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +4W_{\tau}\left|\tilde{h}_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right|\bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{2R}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}\sum_{j=0}^{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}-1}\Bigg(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\bigg(2\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}-\tilde{\psi}_{\mu^{j}}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +4W_{\tau}\left|\tilde{h}_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})-\tilde{h}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i};\mu^{j})\right|\bigg)\Bigg):=\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}},\ \{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+2R\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\tau^{(2)}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\Bigg[2\bigg(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)})}\left\|\psi_{0}(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h})-\psi_{\mu^{j}}(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)})}\left\|\psi_{0}(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h})-\psi_{\mu^{j}}(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h})\right\|_{2}\bigg)+4W_{\tau}\bigg(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)})}\left|h_{0}(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h};\mu^{j})-h(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h};\mu^{j})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)})}\left|h_{0}(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h};\mu^{j})-h(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h};\mu^{j})\right|\bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{2R}{1-\gamma}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}s^{(1)}\sim d^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
a^{(2)}\sim d_{\textup{base}}\end{subarray}}\Bigg[2\bigg(\left\|\psi_{0}(s^{(1)},a^{(1)})-\psi_{\mu^{j}}(s^{(1)},a^{(1)})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\left\|\psi_{0}(s^{(2)},a^{(2)})-\psi_{\mu^{j}}(s^{(2)},a^{(2)})\right\|_{2}\bigg)+4W_{\tau}\bigg(\left|h_{0}(s^{(1)},a^{(1)};\mu^{j})-h(s^{(1)},a^{(1)};\mu^{j})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\ +\left|h_{0}(s^{(2)},a^{(2)};\mu^{j})-h(s^{(2)},a^{(2)};\mu^{j})\right|\bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{2R}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\frac{16W_{\tau}\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}+\frac{40R^{2}W_{\tau}\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}},

where inequality (a) uses Assumption
3.3
.
∎

Let
c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}:=(2+\exp(-2W_{\tau}(\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R))+\exp(2W_{\tau}(\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R)))^{-1}
.

Lemma E.12

(MLE)
.

Assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}
holds. Then, for any phase
n\geq 0
, we have that with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}+\frac{3}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}(\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}])^{2}}\left(\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}+2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}:=\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}.

In other words, defining event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}:=\left\{\left\|\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}}\leq\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}},\ \forall 0\leq n\leq N-1\right\},

we have
\Pr[\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}]\geq 1-2N\delta^{\prime}
.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}\left(17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}+\frac{19c_{\textup{scale}}^{\frac{1}{4}}R^{\frac{5}{4}}M_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\sqrt{1-\gamma}m^{\frac{1}{8}}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right).

Here we make the convention that
\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}:=\zeta_{\textup{HF}}
.

Proof.

Since

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu}^{2}L(\mu)\!

\displaystyle=\!\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)}{\left(1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)\right)^{2}}\!+\!\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu}{\left(1+\exp\left((\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu\right)\right)^{2}}\right)\!\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\!\!\right)

\displaystyle\succeq\frac{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top},

we have that for any
\Delta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\quad\ L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}+\Delta)-L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})^{\top}\Delta

\displaystyle\geq\Delta^{\top}\nabla_{\mu}^{2}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})\Delta

\displaystyle\geq c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\Delta^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)^{\top}\right)\Delta.

Using Lemma
E.11
and the definition of
\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}}
, we have

\displaystyle L(\mu^{n})\leq L(\mu^{*}_{\textup{MLE}})+\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}\leq L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})+\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}.

Then,

\displaystyle\quad\ c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\left(\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)\left(\psi_{0}(\tau^{(1)}_{i})-\psi_{0}(\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)^{\top}\right)\left(\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)

\displaystyle\leq L(\mu^{n})-L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})^{\top}\left(\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})^{\top}\left(\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)+\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}},

which implies

\displaystyle c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\left\|\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}}^{2}\leq\left\|\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}\right)^{-1}}\left\|\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}}+\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}+\frac{4c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}R^{2}}{n}.

By analysis for quadratic functions, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}}\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\left\|\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}\right)^{-1}}+\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}+2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}.

(36)

Let

\displaystyle V_{i}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)},\ \forall i\in[M_{\textup{HF}}],

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle=[V_{1},\dots,V_{M_{\textup{HF}}}]^{\top}\ \in\mathbb{R}^{M_{\textup{HF}}},

\displaystyle X

\displaystyle=[(\psi_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{1},\tau^{(2)}_{1}})^{\top};\dots;(\psi_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{M_{\textup{HF}}},\tau^{(2)}_{M_{\textup{HF}}}})^{\top}]\ \in\mathbb{R}^{M_{\textup{HF}}\times d},

\displaystyle X^{\top}

\displaystyle=[\psi_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{1},\tau^{(2)}_{1}},\dots,\psi_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{M_{\textup{HF}}},\tau^{(2)}_{M_{\textup{HF}}}}]\ \in\mathbb{R}^{d\times M_{\textup{HF}}},

and then

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}V_{i}\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}}=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}X^{\top}V,

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}X^{\top}X+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I.

For any
i\in[M_{\textup{HF}}]
, we have
|V_{i}|\leq 1
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}\left[-\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=1\right\}\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{y_{i}=0\right\}}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{r}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)}\cdot\frac{\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{\exp\left(-\tilde{r}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)}{1+\exp\left(-\tilde{r}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)}\cdot\frac{1}{1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp\left(-\tilde{r}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)-\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)}{\left(1+\exp\left(-\tilde{r}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right)\right)\left(1+\exp\left(-(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)\right)}.

Then,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}]|

\displaystyle\leq\exp(2W_{\tau})\left|(\tilde{\psi}_{0}^{\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i}})^{\top}\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}-\tilde{r}(\tau^{(1)}_{i},\tau^{(2)}_{i})\right|

\displaystyle=\exp(2W_{\tau})\Bigg|\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)}_{i})}f_{0}(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)}_{i})}f_{0}(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ -\left(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)}_{i})}r(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h})-\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)}_{i})}r(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h})\right)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\exp(2W_{\tau})\Bigg(\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)}_{i})}\left|f_{0}(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)}_{i})}\left|f_{0}(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h})\right|\Bigg).

Let
D:=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}X(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}X^{\top}=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}X(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}X^{\top}X+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I)^{-1}X^{\top}\ \in\mathbb{R}^{M_{\textup{HF}}\times M_{\textup{HF}}}
.

Then,

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}\right)^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}\right)^{-1}\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}V^{\top}X\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}\right)^{-1}X^{\top}V

\displaystyle=V^{\top}DV.

Since
D
is positive semi-definite, let
\lambda_{1}\geq\dots\geq\lambda_{M_{\textup{HF}}}\geq 0
denote the eigenvalues of
D
.

We bound
\textup{tr}(D)
,
\left\|D\right\|
,
\textup{tr}\left(D\mathbb{E}[V]\mathbb{E}[V]^{\top}\right)
and
\frac{\left\|D\right\|^{2}}{\textup{tr}\left(D^{2}\right)}
as follows.

\displaystyle\textup{tr}(D)=\textup{tr}\left(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}X\left(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}X^{\top}X+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I\right)^{-1}X^{\top}\right)=\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\textup{tr}\left(\left(X^{\top}X+\frac{M_{\textup{HF}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I\right)^{-1}X^{\top}X\right)\leq\frac{d}{M_{\textup{HF}}},

\displaystyle\left\|D\right\|=\left\|\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}X\left(\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}X^{\top}X+\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I\right)^{-1}X^{\top}\right\|=\left\|\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}}X\left(X^{\top}X+\frac{M_{\textup{HF}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}I\right)^{-1}X^{\top}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{M_{\textup{HF}}},

\displaystyle\textup{tr}\left(D\mathbb{E}[V]\mathbb{E}[V]^{\top}\right)=\textup{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}[V]^{\top}D\mathbb{E}[V]\right)=\mathbb{E}[V]^{\top}D\mathbb{E}[V]\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}[V]\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|D\right\|\leq\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}(\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}])^{2}}{M_{\textup{HF}}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|D\right\|^{2}}{\textup{tr}\left(D^{2}\right)}\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\frac{M_{\textup{HF}}\left\|D\right\|^{2}}{\left(\textup{tr}\left(D\right)\right)^{2}}=\frac{M_{\textup{HF}}\lambda_{1}^{2}(D)}{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\lambda_{i}(D)\right)^{2}}\leq\frac{M_{\textup{HF}}\lambda_{1}^{2}(D)}{\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\lambda_{i}^{2}(D)}\leq M_{\textup{HF}},

where inequality (a) is due to
\textup{tr}\left(D^{2}\right)\geq\frac{(\textup{tr}\left(D\right))^{2}}{M_{\textup{HF}}}
.

Let
\delta^{\prime}\leq\frac{1}{e}
. According to Lemma
F.4
, we have that with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mu}L(\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}\right)^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\textup{tr}\left(D\right)+2\sqrt{\left(\textup{tr}\left(D\right)\right)^{2}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}+2\left\|D\right\|\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\textup{tr}\left(D\mathbb{E}[V]\mathbb{E}[V]^{\top}\right)\left(1+2\sqrt{M_{\textup{HF}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{md}{M_{\textup{HF}}}+\frac{2md}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}+\frac{2}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}(\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}])^{2}}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\left(1+2\sqrt{M_{\textup{HF}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}+3\left(\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}(\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}])^{2}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}.

(37)

Plugging Eq. (
37
) into Eq. (
36
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left\|\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}+3\left(\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}(\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}])^{2}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}+2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}+\frac{3}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}(\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}])^{2}}\left(\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}+2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}:=\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}.

Next, we handle the term
\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}]
. For any
i\in[M_{\textup{HF}}]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}_{i}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\tau^{(2)}_{i}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[(\mathbb{E}_{y_{i}}[V_{i}])^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp(4W_{\tau})W_{\tau}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\tau^{(1)}_{i}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\tau^{(2)}_{i}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}}\Bigg[\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(1)}_{i})}\left(f_{0}(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{(1)}_{i,h},a^{(1)}_{i,h})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau^{(2)}_{i})}\left(f_{0}(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{(2)}_{i,h},a^{(2)}_{i,h})\right)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp(4W_{\tau})W_{\tau}}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}(s^{(1)},a^{(1)})\sim d^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
(s^{(2)},a^{(2)})\sim d_{\textup{base}}\end{subarray}}}\Bigg[\left(f_{0}(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{(1)}_{h},a^{(1)}_{h})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\left(f_{0}(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{(2)}_{h},a^{(2)}_{h})\right)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32R^{2}W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})}{(1-\gamma)m}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}+\frac{12RM_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})}{(1-\gamma)m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}},\ \{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{SGD}}\right]}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}+2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}+\frac{12RM_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})}{(1-\gamma)m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{1}{\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}\left(17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}\left(\frac{40R^{2}W_{\tau}\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}\left(17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +2R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}+\frac{19c_{\textup{scale}}^{\frac{1}{4}}R^{\frac{5}{4}}M_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\sqrt{1-\gamma}m^{\frac{1}{8}}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right).

∎

Lemma E.13
.

Assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}
holds. Then, for any phase
n\geq 0
and iteration
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\quad\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\ \{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}},\ \{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}}\left[\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\right]\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]+\frac{6\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}:=\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\quad\ \left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\left(h(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{n})-r(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\Bigg[\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\Big(h(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{n})-h_{0}(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{n})+h_{0}(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{n})-h_{0}(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})

\displaystyle\quad\ +h_{0}(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s_{h},a_{h})\Big)\Bigg]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\left(h(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{n})-h_{0}(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{n})\right)\right|\right]+\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})^{\top}\left(\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\left(h_{0}(s_{h},a_{h};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s_{h},a_{h})\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\left\|\mu^{n}-\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r}\right\|_{\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}}}\right]+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim d^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|h(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\mu^{n})-h_{0}(^{\prime}s,a^{\prime};\mu^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim d^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|h_{0}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}2\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim d^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|h(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\mu^{n})-h_{0}(^{\prime}s,a^{\prime};\mu^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim d^{\pi^{t}}_{s,a}}\left[\left|h_{0}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|\right],

where inequality (a) uses the definition of
\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}
and Lemma
E.12
.

Then, taking
\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[\cdot]
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim d^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left|h(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\mu^{n})-h_{0}(^{\prime}s,a^{\prime};\mu^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sim d^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left|h_{0}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\mu^{\textup{proj}}_{r})-r(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}2\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]+\frac{2\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\frac{4R}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{m}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]+\frac{6\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}},

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
E.2
and the definition of event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}
.

Furthermore, taking
\mathbb{E}_{\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}},\ \{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}[\cdot]
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\quad\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{l}\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}}\\
\{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}\end{subarray}}\left[\varepsilon^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{MLE}}\right]\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]+\frac{6\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}:=\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}.

∎

In the following, for ease of notation, we use
\mathbb{E}_{\{\tau^{(1)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{n}_{\textup{HF}},\ \{\tau^{(2)}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{M_{\textup{HF}}}\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{\textup{base}}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}}[\cdot]
and
\mathbb{E}_{\hat{r}^{n}}[\cdot]
interchangeably.

Lemma E.14
.

Assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}
holds. Then, for any phase
n\geq 0
, iteration
t\geq 0
,
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
and
a\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|\leq\sqrt{2\beta\left(8(n+1)W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4\zeta_{\textup{cov}}R^{2}\right)}.

Proof.

For any phase
n\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta\right)^{2}-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}},

(38)

Here
W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}
satisfies
\max\{|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)|,|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}|,|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}|\}\leq W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}
.

Plugging
\theta^{t}_{*}
into
\theta
, we have that for any fixed
(s,a)
,

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]-4W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\geq}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]-4W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}},

(39)

where inequality (a) is due to the definition of
\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}
.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}4W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left|Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 8W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}},

(40)

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
F.3
.

On the other hand, it holds that

\displaystyle\ \quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right]}_{\textup{Term $\Gamma^{\textup{NN}}$}\ \geq\ 0},

(41)

where Term
\Gamma^{\textup{NN}}
is non-negative due to the the first-order optimality of
\theta^{t}_{*}
.

Then,

\displaystyle\ \quad(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[\psi_{0}(s,a)\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}](\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 8W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}},

where inequality (a) uses the same argument as Eq. (
17
) (i.e., the first optimality of
\theta^{t}_{*}
).

The above equation implies

\displaystyle\left\|\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})^{\top}\left((n+1)\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}[\psi_{0}(s,a)\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}]+\zeta_{\textup{cov}}I\right)(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})

\displaystyle\leq 8(n+1)W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4\zeta_{\textup{cov}}R^{2}.

For any
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
, using the definition of
\mathcal{K}^{n}
and event
\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\leq\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\hat{\Sigma}^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\leq\sqrt{\beta}.

(42)

Thus, for any
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}})^{-1}}\left\|\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right\|_{\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\beta\left(8(n+1)W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4\zeta_{\textup{cov}}R^{2}\right)}.

∎

E.5
Proof of Theorem
5.2

For any phase
n=0,\dots,N-1
and iteration
t=0,\dots,T-1
, let

\displaystyle\theta^{t}_{*}

\displaystyle:=\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\theta\in\mathcal{S}_{R}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(f_{0}(s,a;\theta)-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;r+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}

\displaystyle:=\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\theta\in\mathcal{S}_{R}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(f_{0}(s,a;\theta)-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right],

\displaystyle\theta^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{SGD}}{\approx}\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\theta\in\mathcal{S}_{R}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(f_{0}(s,a;\theta)-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right].

Let
\delta^{\prime}:=\frac{\delta}{24N(K+M_{\textup{HF}}+M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}+TM^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}})}
.
For any
n\geq 0
,
t\geq 0
and
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
, let
\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a):=b^{n}(s,a)-\mathbb{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[b^{n}(s,a^{\prime})\right]
, and for any
w\in\mathbb{R}^{md}
, let
\bar{\psi}_{w}^{t}(s,a):=\psi_{w}(s,a)-\mathbb{E}_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\psi_{w}(s,a^{\prime})\right]
.

Proof of Theorem
5.2
.

First, we have
\Pr[\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}]\geq 1-6\cdot 2N(K+M_{\textup{HF}}+M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}+TM^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}})\cdot 2\delta^{\prime}=1-\delta
.
In the following, we assume that event
\mathcal{E}_{\textup{init}}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\tau}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\mu}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\cap\mathcal{E}^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{cov}}
holds.

For any phase
n=0,\dots,N-1
and iteration
t=0,\dots,T-1
, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad V_{\mathcal{M}^{n}}^{\pi^{*,n}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V_{\mathcal{M}^{n}}^{\pi^{t}}(s_{\textup{init}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[A_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\bigg[\left(\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}+\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\underbrace{\left(A_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\bar{\psi}^{t}_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+\bar{b}^{n,t}(s,a)\right)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}}_{\textup{Term 1}}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\underbrace{\bar{\psi}^{t}_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}}_{\textup{Term 2}}+\underbrace{\bar{\psi}^{t}_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}}_{\textup{Term 3}}

\displaystyle\ \quad+\underbrace{\left(\bar{\psi}^{t}_{0}(s,a)-\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)\right)^{\top}\theta^{t}}_{\textup{Term 4}}\bigg].

(43)

Below we bound Terms 1-4.

Term 1.

We first bound Term 1.

Term 1

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}},a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a^{\prime})-\left(\psi_{0}(s,a^{\prime})^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a^{\prime})\right)\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*}}}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*}},a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a^{\prime})-\left(\psi_{0}(s,a^{\prime})^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a^{\prime})\right)\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{s_{\textup{init}}}^{\star}}\left[\left(Q_{\mathcal{M}_{b^{n}}}^{\pi^{t}}(s,a)-\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{*}+b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{bias}}},

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
D.2
.

Term 2.

Then, we bound Term 2.

Using Lemma
E.14
, we have that for any
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
and
a\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|\leq\sqrt{2\beta\left(8(n+1)W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4\zeta_{\textup{cov}}R^{2}\right)}.

Thus,

Term 2

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\bar{\psi}^{t}_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}},a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{t}(\cdot|s)}\left[\left|\psi_{0}(s,a^{\prime})^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{*}-\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}\right)\right|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2\beta\left(8(n+1)W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}+4\zeta_{\textup{cov}}R^{2}\right)}.

Term 3.

Next, we bound Term 3.

Using the same argument as Eq. (
41
) (i.e., the first optimality of
\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}
),

\displaystyle\quad\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ -\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}},\hat{r}^{n}}\left[\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\hat{r}^{n}}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\bigg[\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\ -\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\left(Q^{\pi^{t}}(s,a;\hat{r}^{n}+b^{n})-b^{n}(s,a)\right)\right)^{2}\bigg]\bigg|\hat{r}^{n}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\hat{r}^{n}}\left[F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t})-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}})|\hat{r}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\hat{r}^{n}}\left[F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t})-F^{\hat{r}^{n}}(\theta^{t,\hat{r}^{n}}_{\textup{mid}})|\hat{r}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}.

where inequality (a) is due to Lemma
E.9
.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right\|_{\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right)^{\top}\left((n+1)\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\psi_{0}(s,a)\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\right]+\zeta_{\textup{cov}}I\right)\left(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right)

\displaystyle=(n+1)\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}\left[\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\theta^{t}\right)^{2}\right]+4R^{2}\zeta_{\textup{cov}}

\displaystyle\leq(n+1)\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}+4R^{2}\zeta_{\textup{cov}}.

For any
s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}
and
a\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\psi_{0}(s,a)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}\right)^{-1}}\left\|\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right\|_{\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{cov}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\sqrt{2\beta\left((n+1)\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}+4R^{2}\zeta_{\textup{cov}}\right)},

where inequality (a) uses Eq. (
42
).

Hence, we have

Term 3

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\bar{\psi}^{t}_{0}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\theta^{t}_{\textup{mid}}-\theta^{t}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{s\in\mathcal{K}^{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\beta(n+1)\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}}+4R\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}.

Term 4.

Finally, we bound Term 4 as follows.

Term 4

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M}^{n};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*,n}}}\left[\left(\bar{\psi}^{t}_{0}(s,a)-\bar{\psi}^{t}_{w^{t}}(s,a)\right)^{\top}\theta^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*}}}\left[\left|\left(\psi_{0}(s,a)-\psi_{w^{t}}(s,a)\right)^{\top}\theta^{t}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad\ +|\mathcal{A}|\mathbb{E}_{(s,a)\sim d_{\mathcal{M};s_{\textup{init}}}^{\pi^{*}}}\left[\left|\left(\psi_{0}(s,a^{\prime})-\psi_{w^{t}}(s,a^{\prime})\right)^{\top}\theta^{t}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4|\mathcal{A}|\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

The Total Suboptimality.

Combining Lemma
D.3
and Eq. (
43
), we have

\displaystyle V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{t}}(s_{\textup{init}})\leq\textup{RHS in Eq.~\eqref{eq:nn_regret_decomposition}}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{K}^{n}}d^{\pi^{t}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a).

Summing over
t=0,\dots,T-1
, dividing
T
and applying the regret for natural policy gradient (Lemma
E.7
), we have

\displaystyle\ \quad V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{n+1}}(s_{\textup{init}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{t}}(s_{\textup{init}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{(1-\gamma)\eta T}+\frac{\eta W_{S}(W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta})^{2}}{(1-\gamma)}+\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{bias}}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}8\sqrt{\beta(n+1)W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{12R\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{\beta(n+1)\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}}+\frac{4|\mathcal{A}|\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{K}^{n}}d^{\pi^{n+1}}_{s_{\textup{init}}}(s,a).

Next, we handle the term
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sqrt{n+1}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sqrt{(n+1)\varsigma^{\textup{NN},\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{N}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\sqrt{\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{N}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}}+\frac{2N}{\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}\left(17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +4(n+1)R\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}}{n}}+\frac{38Nc_{\textup{scale}}^{\frac{1}{4}}R^{\frac{5}{4}}M_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\sqrt{1-\gamma}m^{\frac{1}{8}}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\Bigg)\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]+\frac{6N\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{N}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}\left(\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{N}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{\sqrt{2N}}{\left(c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}\left(17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +4\sqrt{R}N^{\frac{1}{4}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{7\sqrt{N}c_{\textup{scale}}^{\frac{1}{8}}R^{\frac{5}{8}}M_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}m^{\frac{1}{16}}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\Bigg)\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\!\frac{1}{NT}\!\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\!\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\!\!\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\tau\sim\mathcal{O}^{\pi^{t}}_{\rho^{n}_{\textup{cov}}}}\!\!\left[\left\|\sum_{h=0}^{H(\tau)}\psi_{0}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{(\Sigma^{\textup{NN},n}_{\textup{HF}})^{-1}}\right]}\!+\!\frac{3\sqrt{N}\left(c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\sqrt{1-\gamma}\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}m^{\frac{1}{8}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{N}{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}}\left(\frac{5md\log\left(\frac{N}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M_{\textup{HF}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{\sqrt{2N}}{\left(c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}\left(17W_{\tau}R\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +4\sqrt{R}N^{\frac{1}{4}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{7\sqrt{N}c_{\textup{scale}}^{\frac{1}{8}}R^{\frac{5}{8}}M_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}m^{\frac{1}{16}}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\Bigg)\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\underbrace{\left(2m^{\frac{1}{4}}d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(1+\frac{4NW_{\tau}^{2}}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}md}\right)+\frac{2m^{\frac{1}{4}}d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}(N)}{c_{\textup{base}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)}_{\tilde{d}_{\textup{HF}}}+\frac{3\sqrt{N}\left(c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\sqrt{1-\gamma}\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}m^{\frac{1}{8}}},

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
D.13
with feature dimension
md
.

Recall that
\delta^{\prime}:=\frac{\delta}{24N(K+M_{\textup{HF}}+M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}+TM^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}})}
,
\zeta_{\textup{cov}}:=1
,
\zeta_{\textup{HF}}:=4W_{\tau}^{2}
,
W_{S}:=1
,
W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}:=\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R
,
W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}:=\frac{4}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{4(\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R)}{1-\gamma}
,
W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}:=\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R
,
W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}:=\frac{\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
,
\xi_{\theta}:=\frac{R}{W^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}\sqrt{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}}
,
\xi_{\mu}:=\frac{R}{W_{\tau}\sqrt{M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}}}}
,

\eta:=\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}\sqrt{W_{S}T}}
and

c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}:=(2+\exp(-2W_{\tau}(\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R))+\exp(2W_{\tau}(\sqrt{m}\bar{c}+R)))^{-1}
.
K
and
M_{\textup{HF}}
should satisfy that
K\geq\frac{16(N+1)^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}^{2}}
and
M_{\textup{HF}}\geq\frac{16W_{\tau}^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{2}}
, respectively.

Therefore, summing over
n=0,\dots,N-1
, dividing
N
, and applying Lemma
D.4
, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{\textup{out}}}(s_{\textup{init}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}\left(V^{\pi^{*}}(s_{\textup{init}})-V^{\pi^{n+1}}(s_{\textup{init}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|\varepsilon^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{bias}}}}{1-\gamma}+\underbrace{\frac{\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}{(1-\gamma)\eta T}+\frac{\eta W_{S}(W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta})^{2}}{(1-\gamma)}}_{=\frac{W^{\textup{NN}}_{\theta}\sqrt{W_{S}\log(|\mathcal{A}|)}}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{T}}}+\frac{12R\sqrt{\beta\zeta_{\textup{cov}}}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2md}{(1-\gamma)N\beta}\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\zeta_{\textup{cov}}md}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{8\sqrt{\beta NW^{\textup{NN}}_{\nabla F}R}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{M^{\theta}_{\textup{SGD}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{8\tilde{d}_{\textup{HF}}\sqrt{\beta W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}}}{1-\gamma}\Bigg(\frac{4\sqrt{N}m^{\frac{1}{4}}d^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\frac{N}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}M_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\frac{5\sqrt{N}W_{\tau}^{\frac{1}{4}}R^{\frac{1}{4}}\log^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{(c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}})^{\frac{1}{4}}(M^{\mu}_{\textup{SGD}})^{\frac{1}{8}}}

\displaystyle\quad\ +4\sqrt{R}N^{\frac{1}{4}}\zeta_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{9\sqrt{N}c_{\textup{scale}}^{\frac{1}{8}}R^{\frac{5}{8}}M_{\textup{HF}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(W_{\tau}\exp(4W_{\tau})\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}\sqrt{c^{\textup{NN}}_{\textup{MLE}}}\left(1-\gamma\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}m^{\frac{1}{16}}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\ +\frac{8\sqrt{\beta W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q}}}{1-\gamma}\cdot\frac{3\sqrt{N}\left(c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\sqrt{1-\gamma}\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}m^{\frac{1}{8}}}+\frac{16R\sqrt{\beta N(W^{\textup{NN}}_{f}+W^{\textup{NN}}_{Q})}c_{\textup{scale}}^{\frac{1}{4}}R^{\frac{1}{4}}}{(1-\gamma)\underline{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}m^{\frac{1}{8}}}+\frac{4|\mathcal{A}|\sqrt{c_{\textup{scale}}R^{3}}}{\sqrt{\underline{c}}m^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

(44)

∎

Appendix F
Technical Tools

Lemma F.1
.

Let
\mathcal{D}
be a distribution of random vector
\phi\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
such that
\|\phi\|_{2}\leq W
and
\Sigma=\mathbb{E}_{\phi\sim\mathcal{D}}[\phi\phi^{\top}]
. Given
K
i.i.d. samples
\phi_{1},\dots,\phi_{K}\sim\mathcal{D}
, then with probability at least
1-\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma\right\|\leq\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right].

Proof.

This analysis is originated from Lemma H.3 in
(
Agarwal et al. 2020
)
.

Let
X_{i}=\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma
, and it holds that
\mathbb{E}[X_{i}]=0
and
\|X_{i}\|\leq W^{2}
.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}+\Sigma^{2}-2\Sigma\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right]+\Sigma^{2}-2\Sigma\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right]+\Sigma^{2}-2\Sigma^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right]-\Sigma^{2}.

For any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle x^{\top}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]\right)x=x^{\top}\Sigma^{2}x=\left(\Sigma x\right)^{\top}\Sigma x\geq 0,

which implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right]\succeq\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}^{2}\right].

For any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle x^{\top}\left(W^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right]\right)x

\displaystyle=W^{2}\cdot x^{\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]x-x^{\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)\right]x

\displaystyle\geq W^{2}\cdot x^{\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]x-W^{2}\cdot x^{\top}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]x

\displaystyle=0,

which implies

\displaystyle W^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]\succeq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right].

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]\preceq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right]\preceq W^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}\right]=W^{2}\Sigma,

and thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\preceq KW^{2}\Sigma,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq KW^{4}.

Using the Matrix Bernstein inequality (Theorem 7.7.1 in
(
Tropp et al. 2015
)
), we have that for any
t\geq W^{2}\sqrt{K}+\frac{1}{3}W^{2}
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{K}X_{i}\right\|\geq t\right]\leq 4d\exp\left(\frac{-\frac{1}{2}t^{2}}{W^{4}K+\frac{1}{3}W^{2}t}\right),

which is equivalent to that for any
z\geq\frac{W^{2}}{\sqrt{K}}+\frac{1}{3}\frac{W^{2}}{K}
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}X_{i}\right\|\geq z\right]\leq 4d\exp\left(\frac{-\frac{1}{2}K^{2}z^{2}}{W^{4}K+\frac{1}{3}W^{2}Kz}\right).

Let

\displaystyle z=\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}.

Then,

\displaystyle\ \quad\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}X_{i}\right\|\geq\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4d\exp\left(\frac{-\frac{1}{2}K^{2}\cdot\frac{4W^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{K}}{W^{4}K+\frac{1}{3}WK\cdot\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}}\right)

\displaystyle=4d\exp\left(-\frac{2W^{4}K\log^{2}\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{W^{4}K+\frac{2}{3}W^{3}\sqrt{K}\log\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4d\exp\left(-\log\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\delta^{\prime}.

Thus, with probability at least
1-\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma\right\|\leq\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right].

∎

Lemma F.2
.

For any
n\in[N]
, let
\mathcal{D}^{n}
be a distribution of random vector
\phi\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
such that
\|\phi\|_{2}\leq W
, and define
\Sigma^{n}=\mathbb{E}_{\phi\sim\mathcal{D}^{n}}[\phi\phi^{\top}]
and
\Sigma=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Sigma^{n}
. For any
n\in[N]
, given
K
i.i.d. samples
\phi^{n}_{1},\dots,\phi^{n}_{K}\sim\mathcal{D}^{n}
, let
\hat{\Sigma}^{n}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^{K}\phi^{n}_{j}(\phi^{n}_{j})^{\top}
and
\hat{\Sigma}=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\hat{\Sigma}^{n}
. Letting
K\geq\frac{16N^{2}W^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\zeta^{2}}
, then with probability at least
1-\delta^{\prime}
, we have that for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x\leq x^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x\leq 2x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x.

Proof.

This proof is originated from Lemma H.4 in
(
Agarwal et al. 2020
)
.

According to Lemma
F.1
, we have that for any
n\in[N]
,
with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{N}
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^{K}\phi^{n}_{j}(\phi^{n}_{j})^{\top}-\Sigma^{n}\right\|\leq\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right].

Thus,

\displaystyle\Sigma^{n}-\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I\preceq\frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^{K}\phi^{n}_{j}(\phi^{n}_{j})^{\top}\preceq\Sigma^{n}+\frac{2W^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I,

and then summing over
n\in[N]
, we have

\displaystyle\Sigma-\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I+\zeta I\preceq\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\preceq\Sigma+\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I+\zeta I.

This implies that for
\zeta\geq\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}
,

\displaystyle\left(\Sigma+\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I+\zeta I\right)^{-1}\preceq\left(\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\right)^{-1}\preceq\left(\Sigma-\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I+\zeta I\right)^{-1}.

Let
U\Lambda U^{\top}
be the eigendecomposition of
\Sigma
, where
\Lambda=diag([\lambda_{1},\dots,\lambda_{d}])
and
U=[u_{1},\dots,u_{d}]
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad x^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x-x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x

\displaystyle\leq x^{\top}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x-x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[d]}\left(\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta-\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right)^{-1}-\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta\right)^{-1}\right)\left(u_{i}x\right)^{2}.

Since

\zeta\geq\frac{4NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}
,
we have

\displaystyle 2\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta-\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{i}+\zeta-\frac{4NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}+\sigma_{i}+\zeta

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{i}+\zeta,

and thus

\displaystyle\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta-\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right)^{-1}\leq 2\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta\right)^{-1}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\ \quad x^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x-x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[d]}\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta\right)^{-1}\left(u_{i}x\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\ \quad x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x-x^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x

\displaystyle\leq x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x-x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}I+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[d]}\left(\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta\right)^{-1}-\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta+\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(u_{i}x\right)^{2}.

Since

\zeta\geq\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}
, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{i}+\zeta+\sigma_{i}+\zeta

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{i}+\zeta+\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}},

and thus

\displaystyle\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta\right)^{-1}\leq 2\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta+\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right)^{-1}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\ \quad x^{\top}\left(\Sigma+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x-x^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[d]}\left(\sigma_{i}+\zeta+\frac{2NW^{2}\log\left(\frac{4dN}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{K}}\right)^{-1}\left(u_{i}x\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=x^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}+\zeta I\right)^{-1}x.

∎

Lemma F.3
.

For any
a,b,c\in\mathbb{R}
, we have

\displaystyle(b-a)^{2}-(c-a)^{2}\leq 4\max\{|a|,|b|,|c|\}|b-c|.

Proof.

It holds that

\displaystyle(b-a)^{2}-(c-a)^{2}

\displaystyle=(a^{2}+b^{2}-2ab)-(a^{2}+c^{2}-2ac)

\displaystyle=b^{2}-c^{2}-2a(b-c)

\displaystyle=(b+c)(b-c)-2a(b-c)

\displaystyle=(b+c-2a)(b-c)

\displaystyle\leq 4\max\{|a|,|b|,|c|\}|b-c|.

∎

Lemma F.4

(Theorem 2.1 in
(
Hsu et al. 2012
)
)
.

Let
A\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
be a matrix, and let
\Sigma:=A^{\top}A
. Suppose that
x=(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})
is a random vector such that, for some
\nu\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and
\sigma\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\alpha^{\top}\left(x-\nu\right)\right)\right]\leq\exp\left(\frac{\left\|\alpha\right\|^{2}\sigma^{2}}{2}\right)

for all
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
. For all
t>0
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|Ax\right\|^{2}>\sigma^{2}\left(\textup{tr}(\Sigma)+2\sqrt{\textup{tr}(\Sigma^{2})t}+2\left\|\Sigma\right\|t\right)+\textup{tr}(\Sigma\nu\nu^{\top})\left(1+2\left(\frac{\left\|\Sigma\right\|^{2}}{\textup{tr}\left(\Sigma^{2}\right)}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right]\leq\exp(-t).

Lemma F.5

(Lemma 11 in
(
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
)
.

Let
X_{1},\dots,X_{N}
be a sequence of
d\times d
-dimensional positive semi-definite matrices, and
\|X_{n}\|\leq W_{x}
for all
n\in[N]
. Let
A_{0}=\zeta I_{d}
with
\zeta\geq\max\{1,W_{x}\}
.
For any
n\in[N]
, let
A_{n}=A_{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}
.
Then, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\textup{tr}\left(A_{n-1}^{-1}X_{n}\right)\leq 2\log\left(\frac{\det(A_{N})}{\det(A_{0})}\right).
